## BP008

+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+
| [![BP008F][img/BP008F]][img/BP008F]               | “Figures on the right   |

## BP009

+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+
| [![BP009F][img/BP009F]][img/BP009F]               | “Smooth contour         |

## BP010

+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+
| [![BP010F][img/BP010F]][img/BP010F]               | “Triangles;             |

|                                                   |                         |
|                                                   | [BP010F][url/BP010F]    |
+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+

## BP012

+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+
| [![BP012F][img/BP012F]][img/BP012F]               | “Convex hull of figure  |
|                                                   | elongated; Convex hull  |
|                                                   | of figure compact”      |
|                                                   |                         |
|                                                   | [BP012F][url/BP012F]    |
+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+
| [![BP012E][img/BP012E]][img/BP012E]               | “Thin elongated convex  |

|                                                   | hull.”                  |
|                                                   |                         |
|                                                   | [BP012E][url/BP012E]    |
+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+

## BP013

+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+
| [![BP013F][img/BP013F]][img/BP013F]               | “Vertical rectangles or |
|                                                   | horizontal ellipses;    |
|                                                   | Vertical ellipses or    |

| [![BP013E][img/BP013E]][img/BP013E]               | “Tall rectangle OR wide |

|                                                   | rectangle OR tall       |
|                                                   | ellipse.”               |

|                                                   | [BP013E][url/BP013E]    |
+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+
|                                                   | The rectangles are tall |
|                                                   | and the ellipses are    |
|                                                   | wide on the left, while |

## BP014

+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+
| [![BP014F][img/BP014F]][img/BP014F]               | “Large total line       |

|                                                   | line length”            |
|                                                   |                         |
|                                                   | [BP014F][url/BP014F]    |
+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+
| [![BP014E][img/BP014E]][img/BP014E]               | “All big individual     |
|                                                   | figures vs. all small   |
|                                                   | individual figures.”    |
|                                                   |                         |
|                                                   | [BP014E][url/BP014E]    |
+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+
|                                                   | The left panels contain |
|                                                   | large figures with a    |
|                                                   | long combined line      |
|                                                   | length, while the right |
|                                                   | panels contain small    |
|                                                   | figures with a shorter  |
|                                                   | combined line length.   |

## BP016

+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+
| [![BP016F][img/BP016F]][img/BP016F]               | “Spiral curls           |
|                                                   | counterclockwise;       |
|                                                   | Spiral curls clockwise” |
|                                                   |                         |
|                                                   | [BP016F][url/BP016F]    |
+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+
| [![BP016E][img/BP016E]][img/BP016E]               | “Clockwise spiraling    |
|                                                   | curve vs.               |
|                                                   | counter-clockwise       |
|                                                   | spiraling curve.”       |
|                                                   |                         |
|                                                   | [BP016E][url/BP016E]    |
+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+
|                                                   | Following each spiral   |
|                                                   | from its inner end      |
|                                                   | outward, the curve      |
|                                                   | winds clockwise on the  |
|                                                   | left and                |
|                                                   | counterclockwise on the |
|                                                   | right.                  |
+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+

## BP017

+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+
| [![BP017F][img/BP017F]][img/BP017F]               | “An acute angle         |

## BP018

+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+
| [![BP018F][img/BP018F]][img/BP018F]               | “A neck; No neck”       |
|                                                   |                         |
|                                                   | [BP018F][url/BP018F]    |
+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+
| [![BP018E][img/BP018E]][img/BP018E]               | “"Pinched" shape        |
|                                                   | (drastically thinner    |
|                                                   | somewhere in the middle |
|                                                   | than on the ends) vs.   |
|                                                   | non-pinched shape.”     |
|                                                   |                         |
|                                                   | [BP018E][url/BP018E]    |
+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+
|                                                   | Each left figure        |
|                                                   | narrows to a neck       |
|                                                   | between two wider       |
|                                                   | parts, while the right  |
|                                                   | figures have no such    |
|                                                   | neck.                   |
+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+

[img/BP018F]: img/corrected/BP018F.png
[img/BP018E]: img/corrected/BP018E.png
[url/BP018F]: https://www.foundalis.com/res/bps/bongard/p018.htm
[url/BP018E]: https://oebp.org/BP18

|                                                   | [BP019F][url/BP019F]    |

+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+

+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+

|                                                   | parallel and            |
|                                                   | non-collinear pieces of |
|                                                   | straight lines, each    |
|                                                   | pair with a different   |
|                                                   | slope vs. not so.”      |
|                                                   |                         |
|                                                   | [BP255E][url/BP255E]    |
+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+

|                                                   | three pairs of          |
|                                                   | parallel, offset lines  |
|                                                   | with a different        |
|                                                   | direction for each      |
|                                                   | pair. The right         |

|                                                   | pattern.                |

|                                                   | one; Not so”            |

|                                                   | [BP256F][url/BP256F]    |
+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+

|                                                   | large one than a small  |
|                                                   | one vs. not so.”        |
|                                                   |                         |
|                                                   | [BP256E][url/BP256E]    |
+---------------------------------------------------+-------------------------+

| [![BP257E][img/BP257E]][img/BP257E]               | “The distance from the  |

|                                                   | so.”                    |
|                                                   |                         |
